3. `lowpass_random`: v = sum_{k=1}^{K} amp_k cos(2π k i/n + phase_k), a=exp(v)/sum.
4. `chirp`: v = alpha*(i/n -0.5)^2 + beta*sin(2π gamma i/n + phi), a=exp(v)/sum.
5. if parent: resample parent, reverse; and add noise to log parent.
Use rng.

For small Q, many starts (20). For n=10240, fewer (parent plus 5 random).

 best=a...

      weights top K

Also "active mean" L-BFGS objective can be more powerful. `run_lbfgs_topk`:
```
def topk_obj(a,K):
 d=...
 idx=argpartition(d, K).
 obj=2*mean(d[idx]) + PEN*(S-1)^2
 weights zeros; weights[idx]=1/K
 h=corr_pair(a,w); B=dot(w,c)
 grad=4n h/S² -4nB/S³ +2PEN(S-1)
 return obj, grad
```
This objective is not exactly max but average active; if active set not stable, may not reduce max. But with K=1, it's a single active coefficient (differentiable when index fixed). Random initialization details to avoid all similar:
```
def random_initialize(n, rng):
    mode = rng.integers(0,6)
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if mode==0: # lowpass exp
       K=max(3,min(64,n//4))
       freqs=np.arange(1,K+1)
       amp=1.0/(freqs**1.1) * rng.uniform(0.5,1.5,K)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π,K)
       v=amps cos(...)+...
       return normalize(exp(v))
    elif mode==1: ramp + sine
       alpha=rng.uniform(-1,1); beta=...; v=alpha*x+beta*sin(2π*(x+phi)); return exp(v)
    elif mode==2: sum of Gaussians nonnegative
       m=rng.integers(2,5); centers=rng.uniform(0,1,m); widths=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3,m); weights=rng.uniform(0.5,2,m); a=sum weights exp(-(x-c)^2/(2w²)); return normalize
    elif mode==3: abs sine phases
       f=rng.integers(1,6); phi=rng.uniform(0,1); a=abs(np.sin(2π f x + phi)) + 0.2; return normalize
    elif mode==4: piecewise log random smooth via Fourier random real
    else: (1+α x + β (x-0.5)^2 + noise)
```
Ensure positive: add small 1e-6 and normalize.

   periodically revert to best
```

Now, let's correct derivative and subgradient precisely in code.

`fast_self_conv`: returns c length 2n-1.
`corr_pair`: returns h length n where h_j = ∑_k w_k a_{k-j}. If `w` length 2n-1.
`lse_obj(a,beta)` code robust:
```
def lse_obj(a, beta):
    a=np.asarray(a)
    n=len(a); S=a.sum();
    if S<1e-8: return 1e30, zeros
    c=fast_self_conv(a); cmax... d=n*c/(S*S)
    dmax=d.max(); z=beta*(d-dmax); z=np.clip(z,-700,700); e=np.exp(z); sum_e=e.sum()
    if sum_e<=0: ...
    L=dmax+np.log(sum_e)/beta
    p=e/sum_e
    LDS=np.dot(p,d); B=np.dot(p,c)  # B maybe LDS*n? Since d=n c/S²; B=S²/n * LDS? Simpler B=dot(p,c)
    h=corr_pair(a,p)
    score=2*L + PEN_SUM*(S-1)^2
    grad=4*n*h/(S*S) - 4*n*B/(S*S*S) + 2*PEN*(S-1)
    # Note B=dot(p,c); previous LDS = dot(p,d)=nB/S², so 4LDS/S =4nB/S³.
    return score, grad
```
Check dimensions: For sum1, S=1, grad=4n h -4n B. For uniform active central, h=1/n, B=1/n => grad=0. Great.

But if objective L approximates max d, subgradient of 2L at beta∞: weights active. Derivative 2*(n*2? Wait d=n c/S², ∂d=2n a/S² -2n c/S³. Weighted: 2n h/S² -2n B/S³. Times 2 objective: 4n h/S² -4n B/S³. Yes.

`topk_obj(a,K)`:
```
d=... idx topK weights p; B=dot(p,c); h=corr_pair(a,p); obj=2*mean(d[idx])+PEN*(S-1)^2; grad=4n h/S² -4n B/S³ +2PEN(S-1)
```
If K=1, objective uses active index; if tie, argpartition stable? okay. If active set changes, gradient discontinuity.

`subgrad_step`: For a, compute c,S,d, choose top k weights. `B=dot(w,c)`, `h=corr_pair(a,w)`, `grad=4n h/S² -4nB/S³`. Then exact objective not divided by? To step, use `grad_norm = max(abs(grad))`; parameterize eta = 1e-3*? Multiplicative: `q=normalize(a*np.exp(-eta*grad/gscale))`. This reduces components with positive grad. But gradient can have constant negative? For sum1, grad dot? not necessarily. Use additive: `q=normalize(clip(a - eta*grad/gscale, 0))`. We'll test both.

Potential sign: To minimize, update a <- a - eta * grad. If gradient positive where increasing a increases objective. Good.

`topk` objective can be optimized by L-BFGS. For K active, it effectively minimizes average, which may push non-active up. Use multiple K.

```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution, linprog

PEN_SUM=1e-3

def next_power_of_two(m): ...

def normalize_a(a, target=1.0): ...

def fast_self_conv(a): ...

def corr_pair(a,w): ...

def exact_score(a): ...

def lse_score_grad(a,beta): ...

def topk_score_grad(a,K): ...

def run_lbfgs_lse(a,beta,maxfun): ...

def run_lbfgs_topk(a,K,maxfun): ...

def estimate_eval_time(n): ...

def estimate_maxfun(t_left,n): ...

def resample_sequence(a,n): ...

def upsample_to_len(a,n):
    m=len(a); m=int(m)
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    if n % m == 0:
        return np.repeat(a, n//m)
    # If m divides? exact. Else interp.
    return resample_sequence(a,n)
```
For n multiple, repeat exact. If m > n, interp.

`de_search(q, end_time, seed)`:
```

  bounds=[(-6.0,6.0)]*q
  # choose maxiter based on q and time? For q=24, maxiter=80; pop=15
  if q <= 24: maxiter=100; pop=20
  elif q <= 48: maxiter=60; pop=15
  else: maxiter=40; pop=12
  res=differential_evolution(_de_objective, bounds, maxiter=maxiter, popsize=pop, tol=1e-8, seed=seed, init='so'?)

  return a, exact_score(a)

Let's write `polish_sequence` robust:
```
def polish_sequence(a, end_time, do_lp=False, do_topk=True, do_sub=True, label=''):
    a=normalize_a(np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a)
    n=len(a); t0=time.monotonic(); orig_end=end_time
    if t0 >= orig_end-2: return best_sc,best_a
    total_budget=orig_end - t0
    # LSE stages
    stages = [(20,0.04),(50,0.05),(200,0.10),(1000,0.12),(5000,0.16),(20000,0.18),(100000,0.20),(500000,0.10),(2000000,0.05)]
    work=a.copy(); stall=0
    cum=0.0
    for beta, frac in stages:
        cum += frac
        stage_end = min(orig_end - (0 if not do_sub else 10), t0 + cum*total_budget)
        if time.monotonic() >= stage_end - 1: continue
        while time.monotonic() < stage_end - 1:
            rem=max(1.0, stage_end - time.monotonic())
            maxfun=estimate_maxfun(rem, n)
            if maxfun < 16: break
            sc,a2=run_lbfgs_lse(work,beta,maxfun)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_a=a2.copy(); stall=0
            else:
                stall += 1
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc*1.2:
                work=normalize_a(a2)
            else:
                work=best_a.copy()
            if stall >= 3:
                break
        if time.monotonic() >= orig_end - 20: break
    # topk
    if do_topk and time.monotonic() < orig_end - 10:
        for K in (1,2,3,5,8,15):
            if time.monotonic() >= orig_end - 8: break
            top_end = min(orig_end - (10 if do_sub else 2), time.monotonic() + 5)??? 
```

`corr_pair` implementation:
```
def corr_pair(a,w):
    a=np.asarray...; w=np.asarray...
    n=len(a); m=len(w)
    M=n+m-1; N=next_power_of_two(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]
    return full[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If m not 2n-1? We only call with p length 2n-1. If topk weights length d, yes. Good.

M=2*n-1; N=next_power_of_two(M); A=rfft(a,N); out=irfft(A*A,N)[:M]; return np.maximum(out,0)
```

`polish_many`:
```
def polish_many(starts, end_time, do_lp=False, min_chunk=10.0):
    arr=[]
    for s in starts:
       if s is None: continue
       a=np.asarray...; if a.size==0 continue
       a=normalize_a(a); sc=exact_score(a)
       if finite: arr.append((sc,a))
    if not arr: return inf,None
    # We could pick best initial without sort by scan.
    best_sc=inf; best_a=None

Detailed function implementations:

`next_power_of_two(m)`:
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`fast_self_conv`:
```
def fast_self_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0,dtype=np.float64)
    M=2*n-1
    N=next_power_of_two(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
    out=np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```
If a has negative tiny, we clip before? We can `a=np.maximum(a,0.0)` inside. Good.

`corr_pair` as above; maybe if w length not 2n-1 but for general, return last? We only need. Use `start = n-1`, `count=n`; if full length smaller? full length at least 2n-1 if m>=n? w length 2n-1 => full length 3n-2, start ok.

`normalize_a`:
```
def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, target/float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (target/S)
    return a
```
For target=1.

`exact_score`:
```
def exact_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    a=np.nan_to_num(...); a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    n=a.size; S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <0 or not finite: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*cmax/(S*S))
```
If cmax=0 (all zeros but S>0 impossible) score0? But nonnegative sum>0 => cmax>0. Fine.

`lse_score_grad` careful with `S*S*S`; if S zero return.
```
def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-8:
       return 1e30, np.zeros(n)
    beta=float(beta); if not finite beta<=...: beta=100
    c=fast_self_conv(a)
    d=n*c/(S*S)
    dmax=float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0
    if dmax <=0 or not finite: # still compute h?
    z=beta*(d-dmax); z=np.clip(z,-700,700); e=np.exp(z); sum_e=float(e.sum())
    if sum_e <=0 or not finite: e=np.ones_like(d)/d.size; sum_e=d.size
    p=e/sum_e
    L=dmax + float(np.log(sum_e))/beta
    B=float(np.dot(p,c))
    h=corr_pair(a,p)
    score=2.0*L + PEN_SUM*(S-1.0)**2
    hmax? grad = 4.0*n*h/(S*S) - 4.0*n*B/(S*S*S)
    grad += 2.0*PEN_SUM*(S-1.0)
    grad=np.nan_to_num(...); grad=np.clip(grad,-1e9,1e9)
    return float(score), grad
```
If dmax 0 but S>0, d maybe all zeros? Not possible. But if, p uniform? We set p ones/m.

`topk_score_grad(a,K)`:

K=int(K); if K<1: K=1; if K>d.size: K=d.size
idx=np.argpartition(d,d.size-K)[d.size-K:]
p=zeros; p[idx]=1.0/K
obj=2.0*float(np.mean(d[idx])) + PEN*(S-1)^2

```

`run_lbfgs_lse`:
```
def run_lbfgs_lse(a, beta, maxfun):
    a=normalize_a(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf,a
    if maxfun<16: maxfun=16
    maxiter=max(20, maxfun//2)
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    opts={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-10,'maxiter':int(maxiter),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'maxls':30}
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_score_grad, a, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(float(beta),), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    except Exception:
       return exact_score(a), a
    x=getattr(res,'x',None)
    if x is None: return exact_score(a), a
    x=normalize_a(x)
    sc=exact_score(x)
    if not finite or sc>1e8: sc=exact_score(a); x=a
    return float(sc), x
```
If `res.fun` better but exact worse due LSE, okay.

`run_lbfgs_topk` similar.

def estimate_eval_time(n):
    n=float(n)
    if n < 64: return 2e-5
    if n < 256: return 6e-5
    if n < 1024: return 2e-4
    if n < 4096: return 8e-4
    return 0.002 * max(1.0, n/8192.0)
```

`random_initialize(n, rng)`:
```
def random_initialize(n,rng):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: return np.array([])
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if mode==0:
       K=min(max(3,n//8),64)
       f=np.arange(1,K+1,dtype=float)
       amp=rng.uniform(0.3,1.5,K) / (1+f**0.8)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,K)
       v=np.zeros(n)
       for j in range(K): v += amp[j]*np.cos(2*np.pi*f[j]*x + ph[j])
       a=np.exp(0.5*v) # moderate
    elif mode==1:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(-2,2)); beta=float(rng.uniform(0,3)); phi=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); freq=int(rng.integers(1,5))
       v=alpha*x + beta*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x + phi)
       a=np.exp(0.4*v)
    elif mode==2:
       m=int(rng.integers(2,5)); centers=rng.uniform(0,1,m); widths=rng.uniform(0.05,0.35,m); weights=rng.uniform(0.2,2.0,m)
       a=0.05*np.ones(n)
       for j in range(m): a += weights[j]*np.exp(-((x-centers[j])**2)/(2*widths[j]**2))
    elif mode==3:
       freq=int(rng.integers(1,7)); phi=float(rng.uniform(0,1)); a=np.abs(np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x + phi*np.pi))+0.1
    elif mode==4:
       # Fourier random real smooth
       K=min(max(3,n//16),128); f=np.arange(1,K+1,dtype=float); amp=1.0/(1+f**1.2); ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,K); cph=rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5,K)
       v=0.1? maybe no.
       v=np.zeros(n)
       for j in range(K): v += amp[j]*(np.cos(...)+ cph[j]*np.sin(...))
       a=np.exp(0.5*v)
    else:
       a=1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0,2))*np.abs(x-0.5) + float(rng.uniform(0,1))*(x-0.5)**2 + 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n)
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0.0,posinf=1.0,neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)+1e-6
    return normalize_a(a)
```
For mode4 use phi maybe. In Python, `or` short-circuit in if mode? Already. Good.

`de_search`:

def de_search(q, end_time, seed):
    q=int(q); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    try:
       bounds=[(-6.0,6.0)]*q
       if q<=24: maxiter=120; pop=20
       elif q<=48: maxiter=80; pop=15
       else: maxiter=50; pop=12
       res=differential_evolution(_de_objective, bounds, maxiter=maxiter, popsize=pop, tol=1e-8, seed=seed, init='sobol' if q<=64 else 'latinhypercube') # 'sobol' may require q? Available? To be safe use 'latinhypercube'
       ax=getattr(res,'x',None)
       if ax is not None and finite:
          a=normalize_a(np.exp(np.clip(ax,-20,20)))
          sc=exact_score(a)
          if finite: return a,sc
    except Exception:
       pass
    a=random_initialize(q,rng)
    return a, exact_score(a)
```

`de_objective` top-level:
```
def _de_objective(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v=np.clip(v,-20,20)
    a=np.exp(v)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return 1e100
    a=a/S
    sc=exact_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return 1e100
    return float(sc)
```
Naming with underscore okay top-level.

`polish_many` top-level.
```
def polish_many(starts, end_time, do_lp=False, min_chunk=10.0):
    arr=[]
    for s in starts:
       if s is None: continue
       a=np.asarray(s,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if a.size==0: continue
       a=normalize_a(a)
       sc=exact_score(a)
       if np.isfinite(sc): arr.append((sc,a))
    if not arr: return np.inf, None
    best_sc=min(sc for sc,a in arr?) No lambda; use loop.
    best_a=arr[0][1]
    for sc,a in arr:
      if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now >= end_time - 5: return best_sc,best_a
    max_pol=min(len(arr), max(1, int((end_time-now)//min_chunk)+1))
    # We might want not to polish more than available time; okay.
    for i in range(max_pol):
       if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 8: break
       sc,a=arr[i]
       left_count=max_pol - i - 1
       rem=max(1.0, end_time - time.monotonic())
       if left_count > 0:
          chunk_end=min(end_time - 5, time.monotonic() + 0.95*rem/float(left_count))
       else:
          chunk_end=end_time - 5
       if chunk_end - time.monotonic() < 4.0:
          chunk_end=min(end_time-5, time.monotonic()+5)
       sc2,a2=polish_sequence(a, chunk_end, do_lp=do_lp, do_topk=True, do_sub=True)
       if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc2; best_a=a2
       # also if a2 itself initial? done
    return best_sc,best_a
```

`subgrad_polish` implementation details:
```
def subgrad_polish(a, end_time, kits=(1,2,3,5,8)):
    a=normalize_a(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a)
    n=a.size; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 2 and it < 200:
       it += 1
       S=float(a.sum())
       c=fast_self_conv(a)
       if c.size==0 or S<1e-12: break
       improved=False
       cur=a; cur_sc=best_sc
       for K in kits:
          if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 2: break
          w=active_weights(c,K)
          B=float(np.dot(w,c)); h=corr_pair(a,w)
          grad=4.0*n*h/(S*S) - 4.0*n*B/(S*S*S)
          scale=float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0
          if scale < 1e-15: continue
          for eta in (0.005,0.01,0.03,0.1):
              q=normalize_a(np.clip(a - eta*grad/scale,0.0))
              sc=exact_score(q)
              if finite and sc<best_sc:
                  best_sc=sc; best_a=q.copy(); improved=True; break
              q=normalize_a(a * np.exp(-eta*grad/scale))
              sc=exact_score(q)
              if finite and sc<best_sc:
                  best_sc=sc; best_a=q.copy(); improved=True; break
          if improved: break
       if not improved and it>8: break
       if improved: a=best_a.copy()
    return best_sc,best_a
```
`active_weights` top-level:
```
def active_weights(c,k):
  m=c.size; if k>=m: return np.ones(m)/m
  idx=np.argpartition(c,m-k)[m-k:]
  w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1.0/k; return w
```

def consider_candidate(a, best_sc, best_a, label=''):
    if a is None: return best_sc,best_a
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()

`polish_sequence` code:
```
def polish_sequence(a, end_time, do_topk=True, do_sub=True, label=''):
    a=normalize_a(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    if a.size==0: return np.inf, a
    best_sc=float(exact_score(a)); best_a=a.copy()
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now >= end_time - 2: return best_sc,best_a
    total=max(1.0,end_time-now)
    frac_top=0.15 if do_topk else 0.0
    frac_sub=0.15 if do_sub else 0.0
    frac_lse=max(0.2, 1.0 - frac_top - frac_sub)
    lse_end=now + frac_lse*total
    stages=(...)
    cum=0.0; stall=0; work=best_a.copy()
    for beta,frac in stages:
       cum+=frac
       stage_end=min(lse_end, now + (cum*frac_lse*total))
       if stage_end - time.monotonic() < 2: continue
       while time.monotonic() < stage_end - 1:
          rem=max(1.0, stage_end - time.monotonic())
          maxfun=estimate_maxfun(rem, len(work))
          if maxfun < 16: break
          sc,a2=run_lbfgs_lse(work,beta,maxfun)
          if finite and sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=a2.copy(); stall=0
          else: stall+=1
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc*1.25:
             work=normalize_a(a2)
          else:
             work=best_a.copy()
          if stall >= 3:
             break
       if time.monotonic() >= lse_end: break
    if do_topk and time.monotonic() < end_time - (max(5,0.15*total if do_sub else 0)):
       top_budget=frac_top*total
       top_end=min(end_time - (0 if not do_sub else max(5,frac_sub*total)), time.monotonic() + top_budget)
       work=best_a.copy(); stall=0
       Ks=(1,2,3,5,8,15)
       for K in Ks:
          if time.monotonic() >= top_end - 2: break
          while time.monotonic() < top_end - 2:
              rem=max(1.0, top_end - time.monotonic())
              maxfun=estimate_maxfun(rem,len(work))
              if maxfun < 16: break
              sc,a2=run_lbfgs_topk(work,K,maxfun)
              if finite and sc < best_sc: update; stall=0
              else: stall +=1
              if finite and sc < best_sc*1.15: work=normalize_a(a2)
              else: work=best_a.copy()
              if stall >= 3: break
    if do_sub and time.monotonic() < end_time - 2:
       sub_sc,sub_a=subgrad_polish(best_a, end_time - 2)
       if finite and sub_sc < best_sc: update
    return best_sc,best_a
```

   a=normalize; best...; it=0
   while time < end_time-5 and best_a.size <=1024 and it<5:
      g=solve_lp_one(best_a,end_time)
      if g is None: break
      gnorm=normalize(g)
      best_iter=False
      for cand in [gnorm, normalize(0.5*best+0.5*gnorm), normalize(0.25*best+0.75*gnorm)]:
         sc=exact; if finite and sc<best_sc: update; best_iter=True; break
      if best_iter:
         it+=1
         # quick LSE

            sc2,a2=polish_sequence(best_a,q_end,do_topk=False,do_sub=False,label='lpLSE')
            if sc2<best_sc: update
         # continue maybe

         break
   return best_sc,best_a

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

Algorithm:
- Uses the provided previous sequence as a parent when available.
- Runs coarse global exploration with differential evolution at very small lengths.
- Refines small lengths (64/128/256) with many random smooth starts using a
  log-sum-exp smoothed maximum objective and L-BFGS-B + top-k active objective.
- Performs exact subgradient polishing of active convolution constraints.
- Uses a fixed-parent LP descent heuristic on small lengths to escape basins.
- Progressively refines at 512/1024/2048/4096/10240, then final polish.
- Returns the best sequence found (possibly upsampled when exact/block repetition
  does not hurt).
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution, linprog

PEN_SUM = 1e-3


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """
    Returns h[j] = sum_k w[k] a[k-j] for j=0..n-1, treating a outside [0,n-1] as 0.
    w is usually length 2*n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    M = n + m - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:M]
    full = np.nan_to_num(full, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (float(target) / S)
    return a


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = int(a.size)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S))


def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-8 or S > 1e6:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-6:
        beta = 100.0

    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = float(n) * c / (S * S)
    if d.size == 0:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones_like(d) / float(d.size)
    else:
        z = beta * (d - dmax)
        z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        sum_e = float(e.sum())
        if sum_e <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sum_e):
            p = np.ones_like(d) / float(d.size)
        else:
            p = e / sum_e
            dmax = float(np.max(d))

    L = dmax + float(np.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(np.clip(beta * (d - dmax), -700.0, 700.0)))))) / beta
    B = float(np.dot(p, c))
    h = corr_pair(a, p)
    score = 2.0 * L + PEN_SUM * (S - 1.0) ** 2

    grad = (4.0 * float(n) * h) / (S * S)
    grad -= (4.0 * float(n) * B) / (S * S * S)
    grad += 2.0 * PEN_SUM * (S - 1.0)

    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e9, 1e9)
    return float(score), grad


def topk_score_grad(a, K):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-8 or S > 1e6:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    K = int(K)
    if K < 1:
        K = 1

    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = float(n) * c / (S * S)
    m = int(d.size)
    if m == 0:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if K >= m:
        K = m

    idx = np.argpartition(d, m - K)[m - K:]
    p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    p[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)

    B = float(np.dot(p, c))
    h = corr_pair(a, p)
    obj = 2.0 * float(np.mean(d[idx])) + PEN_SUM * (S - 1.0) ** 2
    grad = (4.0 * float(n) * h) / (S * S)
    grad -= (4.0 * float(n) * B) / (S * S * S)
    grad += 2.0 * PEN_SUM * (S - 1.0)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e9, 1e9)
    return float(obj), grad


def run_lbfgs_lse(a, beta, maxfun):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, a
    maxfun = max(16, int(maxfun))
    maxiter = max(20, maxfun // 2)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 30,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_score_grad,
            a,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(float(beta),),
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return float(exact_score(a)), a

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return float(exact_score(a)), a
    x = normalize_a(x)
    sc = float(exact_score(x))
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e8:
        sc = float(exact_score(a))
        x = a
    return sc, x


def run_lbfgs_topk(a, K, maxfun):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, a
    maxfun = max(16, int(maxfun))
    maxiter = max(20, maxfun // 2)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 30,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            topk_score_grad,
            a,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(int(K),),
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return float(exact_score(a)), a

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return float(exact_score(a)), a
    x = normalize_a(x)
    sc = float(exact_score(x))
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e8:
        sc = float(exact_score(a))
        x = a
    return sc, x


def estimate_eval_time(n):
    n = float(n)
    if n < 64.0:
        return 5e-5
    if n < 256.0:
        return 2e-4
    if n < 1024.0:
        return 6e-4
    if n < 4096.0:
        return 2e-3
    if n < 10000.0:
        return 5e-3
    return 0.012


def estimate_maxfun(t_left, n):
    t_left = max(1.0, float(t_left))
    est = max(1e-5, estimate_eval_time(n))
    mf = int((0.5 * t_left) / est)
    return max(24, min(5000, mf))


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)
    return np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)


def upsample_to_len(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if n > m and (n % m) == 0:
        return np.repeat(a, n // m)
    return resample_sequence(a, n)


def random_initialize(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if mode == 0:
        K = min(max(3, n // 8), 64)
        f = np.arange(1, K + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        amp = rng.uniform(0.3, 1.5, size=K) / (1.0 + f ** 0.8)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=K)
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(K):
            v += amp[j] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f[j] * x + ph[j])
        a = np.exp(0.5 * v)
    elif mode == 1:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 3.0))
        phi = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        v = alpha * x + beta * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phi)
        a = np.exp(0.4 * v)
    elif mode == 2:
        m = int(rng.integers(2, 5))
        centers = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, size=m)
        widths = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35, size=m)
        weights = rng.uniform(0.2, 2.0, size=m)
        a = 0.05 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(m):
            a += weights[j] * np.exp(-((x - centers[j]) ** 2) / (2.0 * widths[j] ** 2))
    elif mode == 3:
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
        phi = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)) * np.pi
        a = np.abs(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phi)) + 0.1
    elif mode == 4:
        K = min(max(3, n // 16), 128)
        f = np.arange(1, K + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        amp = 1.0 / (1.0 + f ** 1.2)
        phc = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=K)
        phs = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=K)
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(K):
            v += amp[j] * (np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f[j] * x + phc[j]) +
                           0.6 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f[j] * x + phs[j]))
        a = np.exp(0.5 * v)
    else:
        a = (1.0 +
             float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0)) * np.abs(x - 0.5) +
             float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)) * (x - 0.5) ** 2 +
             0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n))

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0) + 1e-6
    return normalize_a(a)


def _de_objective(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.clip(v, -20.0, 20.0)
    a = np.exp(v)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return 1e100
    a = a / S
    sc = exact_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e100
    return float(sc)


def de_search(q, end_time, seed):
    q = int(q)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    try:
        bounds = [(-6.0, 6.0)] * q
        if q <= 24:
            maxiter = 120
            popsize = 20
        elif q <= 48:
            maxiter = 80
            popsize = 15
        else:
            maxiter = 50
            popsize = 12
        res = differential_evolution(
            _de_objective,
            bounds,
            maxiter=int(maxiter),
            popsize=int(popsize),
            tol=1e-8,
            seed=int(seed),
            polish=False,
        )
        ax = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if ax is not None:
            ax = np.asarray(ax, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if ax.size == q and np.all(np.isfinite(ax)):
                a = normalize_a(np.exp(np.clip(ax, -20.0, 20.0)))
                sc = float(exact_score(a))
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    return a, sc
    except Exception:
        pass
    a = random_initialize(q, rng)
    return a, float(exact_score(a))


def active_weights(c, k):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(c.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(k)
    if k <= 0 or k >= m:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, m - k)[m - k:]
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def subgrad_polish(a, end_time, kits=(1, 2, 3, 5, 8)):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf, a
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(exact_score(a))
    n = int(a.size)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < max(0.0, end_time - 2.0) and it < 200:
        it += 1
        S = float(a.sum())
        if S < 1e-12:
            break
        c = fast_self_conv(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break

        improved = False
        for K in kits:
            if time.monotonic() >= max(0.0, end_time - 2.0):
                break
            w = active_weights(c, K)
            if w.size != c.size:
                continue
            B = float(np.dot(w, c))
            h = corr_pair(a, w)
            grad = (4.0 * float(n) * h) / (S * S)
            grad -= (4.0 * float(n) * B) / (S * S * S)
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
            if scale < 1e-15:
                continue

            for eta in (0.005, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1):
                q1 = normalize_a(np.maximum(a - eta * grad / scale, 0.0))
                sc1 = float(exact_score(q1))
                if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc1
                    best_a = q1.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break

                tmp = a * np.exp(-eta * grad / scale)
                tmp = np.maximum(tmp, 1e-300)
                q2 = normalize_a(tmp)
                sc2 = float(exact_score(q2))
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = q2.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if not improved and it > 8:
            break
        if improved:
            a = best_a.copy()

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def polish_sequence(a, end_time, do_topk=True, do_sub=True, label=""):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf, a
    best_sc = float(exact_score(a))
    best_a = a.copy()

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= max(0.0, end_time - 2.0):
        return best_sc, best_a

    total = max(1.0, float(end_time - now))
    frac_top = 0.15 if do_topk else 0.0
    frac_sub = 0.15 if do_sub else 0.0
    frac_lse = max(0.2, 1.0 - frac_top - frac_sub)
    lse_end = now + frac_lse * total

    stages = [
        (20.0, 0.04),
        (50.0, 0.05),
        (200.0, 0.10),
        (1000.0, 0.12),
        (5000.0, 0.16),
        (20000.0, 0.18),
        (100000.0, 0.20),
        (500000.0, 0.10),
        (2000000.0, 0.05),
    ]

    work = best_a.copy()
    stall = 0
    cum = 0.0
    for beta, frac in stages:
        cum += frac
        stage_end = min(lse_end, now + (cum * frac_lse * total))
        if time.monotonic() >= stage_end - 1.0:
            continue
        while time.monotonic() < stage_end - 1.0:
            rem = max(1.0, float(stage_end - time.monotonic()))
            maxfun = estimate_maxfun(rem, int(work.size))
            if maxfun < 16:
                break
            sc, a2 = run_lbfgs_lse(work, float(beta), maxfun)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = np.asarray(a2, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1

            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < max(1.0, best_sc * 1.25):
                work = normalize_a(a2)
            else:
                work = best_a.copy()

            if stall >= 3:
                break
        if time.monotonic() >= lse_end - 1.0:
            break

    if do_topk and time.monotonic() < max(0.0, end_time - 3.0):
        reserve = max(5.0, frac_sub * total) if do_sub else 2.0
        top_budget = frac_top * total
        top_end = min(max(0.0, end_time - reserve), time.monotonic() + top_budget)
        work = best_a.copy()
        stall = 0
        for K in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 15):
            if time.monotonic() >= top_end - 2.0:
                break
            while time.monotonic() < top_end - 2.0:
                rem = max(1.0, float(top_end - time.monotonic()))
                maxfun = estimate_maxfun(rem, int(work.size))
                if maxfun < 16:
                    break
                sc, a2 = run_lbfgs_topk(work, int(K), maxfun)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_a = np.asarray(a2, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    stall += 1

                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < max(1.0, best_sc * 1.15):
                    work = normalize_a(a2)
                else:
                    work = best_a.copy()
                if stall >= 3:
                    break

    if do_sub and time.monotonic() < max(0.0, end_time - 2.0):
        sc_sub, a_sub = subgrad_polish(best_a, max(0.0, end_time - 2.0))
        if np.isfinite(sc_sub) and sc_sub < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_sub)
            best_a = np.asarray(a_sub, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def solve_lp_one(a, end_time):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0 or n > 1024:
        return None
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return None
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    M = M * (1.0 + 1e-6) + 1e-12

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo <= hi:
            A[k, lo:hi + 1] = a[k - hi:k - lo + 1]

    b = M * np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    t_left = max(1.0, float(max(0.0, end_time - time.monotonic())))
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={
                "presolve": "high",
                "time_limit": int(max(2, t_left - 2.0)),
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    if x.size != n:
        return None
    if float(x.sum()) <= 1.0 + 1e-5:
        return None
    return x


def lp_improve(a, end_time):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf, a
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(exact_score(a))
    it = 0

    while (time.monotonic() < max(0.0, end_time - 8.0) and
           best_a.size <= 1024 and
           it < 5):
        it += 1
        g = solve_lp_one(best_a, max(0.0, end_time - 2.0))
        if g is None:
            break

        gnorm = normalize_a(g)
        cands = [
            gnorm,
            normalize_a(0.5 * best_a + 0.5 * gnorm),
            normalize_a(0.25 * best_a + 0.75 * gnorm),
        ]
        improved = False
        for cand in cands:
            sc = float(exact_score(cand))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            break

        if time.monotonic() < max(0.0, end_time - 12.0):
            q_end = min(max(0.0, end_time - 6.0), time.monotonic() + 15.0)
            q_sc, q_a = polish_sequence(best_a, q_end, do_topk=False, do_sub=False, label="lp-lse")
            if np.isfinite(q_sc) and q_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(q_sc)
                best_a = np.asarray(q_a, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def consider_candidate(a, best_sc, best_a, label=""):
    if a is None:
        return best_sc, best_a
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_sc, best_a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    sc = float(exact_score(a))
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < float(best_sc):
        print("New best {:.8f} len={} {}".format(sc, int(a.size), label))
        return float(sc), normalize_a(a)
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def make_starts_for_length(Q, best_a, parent, rng, n_random, include_variants=True):
    Q = int(Q)
    starts = []
    if best_a is not None:
        b = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if b.size > 0:
            starts.append(upsample_to_len(b, Q))
            if include_variants:
                p = normalize_a(b)
                noisy = p * np.exp(0.15 * rng.standard_normal(Q))
                starts.append(normalize_a(np.maximum(noisy, 0.0)))

    if parent is not None:
        p = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, Q))
        starts.append(p)
        if include_variants:
            starts.append(p[::-1].copy())
            noisy = p * np.exp(0.10 * rng.standard_normal(Q))
            starts.append(normalize_a(np.maximum(noisy, 0.0)))

    for _ in range(max(0, int(n_random))):
        starts.append(random_initialize(Q, rng))

    return starts


def polish_many(starts, end_time, do_lp=False, min_chunk=10.0):
    arr = []
    for s in starts:
        if s is None:
            continue
        a = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            continue
        a = normalize_a(a)
        sc = float(exact_score(a))
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            arr.append((sc, a))

    if not arr:
        return np.inf, None

    best_sc = float("inf")
    best_a = None
    for sc, a in arr:
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= max(0.0, end_time - 6.0):
        return float(best_sc), best_a

    # Put the best initial candidate first.
    best_index = 0
    best_initial = float(arr[0][0])
    for i in range(1, len(arr)):
        if float(arr[i][0]) < best_initial:
            best_initial = float(arr[i][0])
            best_index = i

    ordered = [arr[best_index]]
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        if i != best_index:
            ordered.append(arr[i])

    remaining0 = max(1.0, float(end_time - now))
    max_pol = min(len(ordered), max(1, int(remaining0 // max(1.0, float(min_chunk))) + 1))
    selected = ordered[:max_pol]

    if len(selected) == 1:
        chunks = [remaining0]
    else:
        first = 0.60 * remaining0
        rest = max(0.0, remaining0 - first)
        n_rest = len(selected) - 1
        chunk_rest = rest / float(n_rest) if n_rest > 0 else 0.0
        chunks = [first] + [chunk_rest] * n_rest

    for i in range(len(selected)):
        if time.monotonic() >= max(0.0, end_time - 6.0):
            break
        sc0, a0 = selected[i]
        rem = max(0.0, float(end_time - time.monotonic()))
        if rem <= 4.0:
            break
        chunk_end = min(max(0.0, end_time - 3.0), time.monotonic() + float(chunks[i]))
        if chunk_end <= time.monotonic() + 4.0:
            chunk_end = min(max(0.0, end_time - 3.0), time.monotonic() + max(5.0, float(chunks[i])))
        sc, a = polish_sequence(a0, chunk_end, do_topk=True, do_sub=True, label="polish")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    if do_lp and best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < max(0.0, end_time - 25.0):
        sc_lp, a_lp = lp_improve(best_a, max(0.0, end_time - 2.0))
        if np.isfinite(sc_lp) and sc_lp < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_lp)
            best_a = np.asarray(a_lp, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(min(2400.0, max(60.0, float(budget_s))))
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 40.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if h.size >= 32 and float(h.sum()) >= 0.01:
                parent = h
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_sc = float("inf")
    best_a = None

    if parent is not None:
        p = normalize_a(parent)
        best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(p, best_sc, best_a, "parent-orig")
        for Q in (2048, 4096):
            pQ = resample_sequence(parent, Q)
            best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(pQ, best_sc, best_a, "parent-g{}-{}".format(Q, "resample"))
    else:
        a0 = random_initialize(2048, rng)
        best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(a0, best_sc, best_a, "random-init")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = normalize_a(np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64))
        best_sc = float(exact_score(best_a))

    print("Initial best {:.8f} len={}".format(best_sc, int(best_a.size)))

    # Phase 1: coarse differential evolution.
    phase1_end = min(deadline, t_start + min(70.0, 0.03 * total_budget))
    if time.monotonic() < phase1_end - 25.0:
        for Q in (24, 32):
            if time.monotonic() > phase1_end - 30.0:
                break
            s = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
            a_de, sc_de = de_search(Q, phase1_end, s)
            best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(a_de, best_sc, best_a, "de-q{}".format(Q))
            sub_end = min(phase1_end, time.monotonic() + 25.0)
            if time.monotonic() < sub_end - 3.0:
                sc_p, a_p = polish_sequence(a_de, sub_end, do_topk=True, do_sub=False, label="de-polish")
                best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(a_p, best_sc, best_a, "de-polish-q{}".format(Q))

    # Phase 2: small lengths.
    phase2_end = min(deadline, t_start + min(560.0, 0.23 * total_budget))
    small_lengths = [64, 128, 256]
    for idx, Q in enumerate(small_lengths):
        if time.monotonic() >= phase2_end - 40.0:
            break
        rem = float(phase2_end - time.monotonic())
        count = len(small_lengths) - idx
        q_end = min(phase2_end, time.monotonic() + max(40.0, rem / float(count)))
        n_random = 12 if Q <= 128 else 8
        starts = make_starts_for_length(Q, best_a, parent, rng, n_random, include_variants=True)
        sc, a = polish_many(starts, q_end, do_lp=(Q <= 256), min_chunk=8.0 if Q <= 128 else 12.0)
        best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(a, best_sc, best_a, "small-q{}".format(Q))

    # Phase 3: mid lengths.
    phase3_end = min(deadline, t_start + min(1120.0, 0.46 * total_budget))
    mid_lengths = [512, 1024, 2048]
    for idx, Q in enumerate(mid_lengths):
        if time.monotonic() >= phase3_end - 70.0:
            break
        rem = float(phase3_end - time.monotonic())
        count = len(mid_lengths) - idx
        q_end = min(phase3_end, time.monotonic() + max(70.0, rem / float(count)))
        n_random = 4 if Q <= 1024 else 2
        starts = make_starts_for_length(Q, best_a, parent, rng, n_random, include_variants=(Q <= 1024))
        sc, a = polish_many(starts, q_end, do_lp=(Q <= 1024), min_chunk=40.0)
        best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(a, best_sc, best_a, "mid-q{}".format(Q))

    # Phase 4: 4096.
    phase4_end = min(deadline, t_start + min(1560.0, 0.65 * total_budget))
    if time.monotonic() < phase4_end - 80.0:
        starts = make_starts_for_length(4096, best_a, parent, rng, 1, include_variants=False)
        sc, a = polish_many(starts, phase4_end, do_lp=False, min_chunk=90.0)
        best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(a, best_sc, best_a, "long-q4096")

    # Phase 5: 10240 (or parent length if larger).
    target_long = 10240
    if parent is not None and parent.size > target_long:
        target_long = int(parent.size)
    phase5_end = min(deadline, t_start + min(2320.0, 0.965 * total_budget))
    if time.monotonic() < phase5_end - 80.0:
        starts = make_starts_for_length(target_long, best_a, parent, rng, 1, include_variants=False)
        sc, a = polish_many(starts, phase5_end, do_lp=False, min_chunk=120.0)
        best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(a, best_sc, best_a, "long-q{}".format(target_long))

    # Final polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        sc, a = polish_sequence(best_a, deadline, do_topk=True, do_sub=True, label="final")
        best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(a, best_sc, best_a, "final-polish")

    # Try exact upsample to a long length when it preserves score.
    if best_a is not None:
        target = 10240
        m = int(best_a.size)
        if m > 0 and m != target and target % m == 0:
            up = upsample_to_len(best_a, target)
            sc_up = float(exact_score(up))
            if np.isfinite(sc_up) and sc_up <= best_sc + 1e-7:
                best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(up, best_sc, best_a, "upsample-10240")
        elif m > 0 and m != target:
            up = resample_sequence(best_a, target)
            sc_up = float(exact_score(up))
            if np.isfinite(sc_up) and sc_up < best_sc - 1e-9:
                best_sc, best_a = consider_candidate(up, best_sc, best_a, "resample-10240")

    # Cleanup and return.
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if out_arr.size == 0:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    verify_deadline = t_start + total_budget - 3.0
    verified = None
    if time.monotonic() < verify_deadline - 10.0:
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = None

    print("FINAL internal={:.8f} verified={} len={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        float(best_sc),
        "{:.8f}".format(verified) if verified is not None else "skipped",
        len(out),
        time.monotonic() - t_start,
    ))
    return out
```
